# Documentation inventory snapshot

Generated by `SnippetCompilationTests.Documentation_FrozenSurface`. Do not edit
below the marker by hand.

The test classifies every documentation block and freezes the SHA-256 of each
inventoried file and block. Any change to `README.md`, `docs/**`, `src/**/*.md`,
or the inventoried sample sources fails the test until this snapshot is
regenerated. Review the changed surfaces, then run:

```bash
AAUTH_UPDATE_DOCS_INVENTORY=1 dotnet test tests/AAuth.Tests --filter "FullyQualifiedName~Documentation_FrozenSurface"
```

Rerun without the variable to confirm it passes, and commit this file with the
documentation change.

<!-- generated-docs-surface -->

175 files; 707 blocks. Counts by validation class:
